DELHI DEVELOPMENT AUTHORITY 
New Delhi, the 25th September 1970 

(61 of 1957 ) 
CONSTITUTED UNDER THE DELHI DEVELOPMENT ACT , 1957 

S . O . 1135 .- - In exercise of the powers conferred 
by section 57 of the Delhi Development Act , 1957 (61 
of 1957 ) , the Delhi Development Authority with the 
previous approval of the Central Government, hereby 
makes the following regulations , namely : 

1. Short title and application .- (1) These regulations 
may be called the Delhi Development Authority ( Issue 
and Management of Bonds) Regulations, 1970 , 

( ii) They shall apply to bond issued and sold by 
the Authority under sub - section ( 3 ) of Section 23 of 
the Delhi Development Act, 1937. 

2 . Definitions. In these regulations unless there is 
anything repugnant in the subject or context , 
(a ) " The Act " means the Delhi Development Act, 

1957; 
(b ) " The Bank ” means the State Bank of India ; 
( c ) " Bond " means the bonds issued and sold by 

the Authority under sub - section ( 5 ) of sec 

tlon 23 of the Act ; 
( d ) " Authority " means the Delhi Development 

Authority constituted under the Delhi Deve 
lopment Act, 1957 (81 of 1957 ) , 


( C ) " Defaced Bond " means a bond which has 

been made illegible and rendered undeci 
pherable in material parts and the material 

parts of a bond are those where : - - 
( i) the number , the issue to which it apper 

tains and the face value of the bond or 

payments of interest are recorded , or 
( ii) the endorsement or the name of the payee 

is written , or 
(iii) the renewal receipt is supplied . 
( 1 ) " Form " means a form as set out in the sche 

dule to these regulations, 
( g ) " Lost Bond " means a bond which has 

actually been lost and shall not mean a bond 
which is in possession of some person adver 

sely to the claimant ; 
( h ) " Mutilated Bond " means a bond which has 

been destroyed , torn or damaged in material 
parts thereof ; 
" Omce of Issue" means the Office of the State 
Bank of India , Parliament Street , New Delhi / 
Office of the Delhi Development Authority or 
the books of which a bond is registered or 

may be registered , as the case may be ; 
( 1) " Prescribed Officer " means such Officers of 

the Authority or the Bank as may be autho 
rised by it for the purposes of Regulations 
9 , 10 , 11, 13, 14 and 15 .

11. Determination of a mutilated bond 18 à bond 
requiring renewal. - It shall be at the option of the

12 . When a bond is required to be renewed . - ( 1) A 
holder of a bond may be required by the office of 
issue to receipt the same for renewal in any of the 
following cases, namely : 
( a ) if only sufficient room remains on the back 

of the bond for one further endorscnient or 
if any word is written upon the bond across 

the existing endorsement or endorsements ; 
( b ) if the bond is torn or any way damaged or 

crowded with writing or unft , in the opinion 

of the office of issuc . 
( c ) if any endorsement is not clear and distinct or 

does not indicate the payee or payees , as the 
case may be , by name or is made otherwise 
than in one of the endorsement cages on the 

back of the bond ; 
( a ) if the Interest on the bond has remained 

undrawn for ten years or more ; 
( e ) if the interest cages on the reverse of the 

bond have been completely fillcd or if the 
vacant prescribed cages on the reverse of the 
bond do not correspond with the half years 
for which interest has become due on the date 
when the bond is presented for drawal of 

Interest ; 
( 1 ) if the bond having been enfaced three times 

for payment of interest is presented for re 

enfacement; and 
( g ) it in the opinion of the office of issue, the 

title of the person presenting the bond for 
payment of interest is irregular or not fully 

proved 
( 2 ) When requisition for renewal of a bond has 
been made under sub - regulation ( 1 ) payment of any 
further interest thereon shall be refused until it is re 
ceipted for renewal 2nd actually renewed .

10 . Publication of list . ( 1 ) The list referred to in 
regulation 9 shall be published half yearly in the 
Gazette of India in the months of January and July 
or as soon afterwards as may be convenient , 

( 2 ) All bonds in respect of which an order has been 
passed under regulation 9 shall be included in the 
Arst list published next after the passing of such order 
and thereafter suich bonds shall continue to be includ 
ed in every succeeding list until tre expiration of 
six years from the date of first publication . 

( 3 ) The list shall contain the followinig particulars 
regarding cach bond included therein , namely , the 
name of the issue, the number of the bond , its value, 
the name of the person to whom it was issucd . the 
date from which it bear s interest, fue name of the 
applicant for a duplicate the fiumber and date of the 


13. Person whose title to a bond of a deceased sole 
holder may be recognised . - ( 1 ) The executors or ad 
ministrators of a decrased sole holder of a bond (whe 
ther a Hindu, Mohammedan , Parsi or otherwise ) and 
the holder of a succession certificate issued under 
Part X of the Indinn Succession Act, 1925 ( XXXIX of 
1926 ) in respect of the bond shall be the only persons 
who may be recognised by the office of issue ( subject 
to any general or special instructions of the pres 
cribed officer ) as having any title to the bond . 

( 2 ) Notwithstanding anything contained in Section 
45 of the Indian Contract Act, 1872 ( IX of 1872 ) , in 
the case of a bond issued , sold or held payable to 
two or more holders , the survivors or survivor and 
on the death of the last survivor, his cxecutors, admi 
nistrators , or any person who is the holder of a succes 
sion certificate in respect of such hond shall be the 
only person who may be recognised by the office of 
issue ( subject to any general or special instructions 
of the prescribed officer ) as having any title to the 
bond . 

( 3 ) The office of issuç shall not be bound to re

from a competent court or oflice in India , having

14 . Receipt for renewal etc . — ( 1 ) Subject to any 
general or special instructions of the prescribed 
officer , the office of issue, by its order , on the appli 
cation of the holder , on his delivering the bond or 
bonds and on his satisfying the office of issue regard 
ing the justice of his claim , renew , sub - divide or con 
solidate a bond or bonds provided the bond or bonds 
has or have been receipted in Form I, II, or III as 
the case may be. 

( 2 ) The office of issue may , under the orders of 
prescribed officer , require the applicant for renewal, 
sub - division or consolidation of a bond under the sub 
regulation ( 1 ) to execute a bond in Form IV with one 
or more sureties approved by him . 

15 . Renewal of bond in case of dispute as to title . 
Where there is a dispute as to the title to a bond in 
respect of which an application for renewal has been 
made, the prescribed officer may : 
( a ) where any party to the dispute has obtained 

A final decision from a court of competent 
jurisdiction declaring him to be entitled to 
such bond , issue a renewed bond in favour 

of such party ; or 
(b ) refuse to renew the bond until such a deci 

słon has been obtained . 
Explanation .- - For the purposes of this suh - regulation . 
the expression final decision means a decision which 
is not appealable or a decision which is appealnhle but 
against which no appeal has been pled within the 
period of limitation allowed by law . 

16. Liability in respect of bond renewed etc . — When 
a duplicate bond has been issued under regulation 9 
or a renewcd bond has been issued or a new hond 
has been isgued upon sub - division or consolidation 
under regulation 14, in favour of a person , the hond 
so issued shall be deemed to constitute a new contract 
between the Authority and such person and A ] ] per 
sons deriving title thereafter through him . 

17. Discharge. The Authority shall be discharged 
from all liability in respect of the bond or hands paid 
on maturity or in place of which a duplicate , renewed , 
suh - divided or consolidated bond or honds has or havo 
been issued : - - 
( a ) in the case of payment, after the lapse of six 

years from the date on which payment was 

due : 
( b ) in the case of a dunlicate hond after the lange 

of six years from the date of the publication 
under regulntion 10 of the list in which the 
hond is first mentioned , or from the date of 
the payment of interest on the original bond , 

whichever date is later ; 
( c ) in the case of a renewed hond or of a new 

hond issued upon - sub - division or consolida 
tion after the lapse of six years from the date 

of issue thereof. 
18 . Discharge in respect of interest, Save as other 
wise expreogly provided in the terms of the hond , no 
person shall be entitled to claim interest on any such 
hond in respect of any period which has elapsed after 
the earliest date on which remand could have hern 
mode for the payment of the amount due on such 
hond . 

19 . Discharge of a hond - When a hond becomes due 
for nayment of prinrinal, the hond shall be presented 
at the office of the Bank at which the Interest thereon 
to payable or at the office of issue duly signed hy the 
holder on its reverse ,

rentahle to the Authority as security otherwise re 
quired to he denncited in each hy its emplovees , non 
tractors of sunpliers and other persons who might 
transect with the Authority .

JUDGMENT: (Oral) , 

By my order dated November 15 , 1971 , I had found 
that the selected ballot papers do not contain any of 
the characteristics which were alleged by the petitioner 
and the inspection of the ballot papers must rest there . 
The request of the petitioner for further chemical exa 
mination was therefore, rejected and it was directed 
that the pctitioner will not be entitled to lead any 
evidence on Issues Nos . 8 to 14 . 

This petition has been listed for today for recording 
evidence on behalf of the petitioner on Issues Nos . 1 
to 7 which related to other corrupt practices . The

of thc petitioner that there has been no burgain with 
and consideration passed between him and respondent 
No, I . 

In view of section , 110 of the Representation to the 
People Act, 1951, I had asked the learned counsel for 
both sides to show me cases to the effect that the pro 
visions of the Code of Civil Procedure will apply to 
the trial of an elcction petition including the provisions 
of dismissal of an election petition for detault unless 
there is a provision contrary thereto in the said Art . I 
had asked them to do so because in the event on elec 
tion petitioner applies for withdrawal. an claborate 
procedure etailed in section 110 of the said Act has 
to be gone through . 

Counsel on both sides have cited to me the cases re 
ported in A . I. R . 1958 Madhya Pradesh 200 in re : Sunder 
Tal Mannalal v . Nandranidas Dwarkadas and others ; 
HIR . 1965 Patna 378 in re: Sawrlia Behari Lall Verma 
v . Trihikram . Deo Naran Singh and others and A . I. R . 
1968 Punjab and Haryana 152 in re : Jugul Kishorr v . 
Doctor Buldor Prakash in which it has been held that 
an election petition can be dismissed for default of 
appearance. The situation that has arised hefore me 
is not that the petitioner has defaulted in appearance 
hut that having appeared , he has stated that he is not 
interested in leading cvidence on Issues Nos . 1 to 7 
because he would like to agitate the question rotating 
to Issues Nos . 8 to 14 in the Supreme court where he 
had Aled a petition for special lenve to appeal arainst 
my order dated November 15 , 1971 which had been 
withdrawn in the circumstances stated by the petitioner 
in hi ; statement today . 


gave rise to this Issue was connected with the Issuos 
relnting to fraudulent ballot papers . Therefore , no 
order is necessary on this Issue , 

Issue No , 16 also depends on whether or not a lac 
or more fraudulant votes were counted for respondent 
No . 1 in the election and this Issue is also , therefore , 
connected with the Issues relating to fraudulent ballot 
papers and falls with Issues Nos . 8 to 14 , 

The corrupt practice which were the subject, matter 
of the Issues framed by me were as follows : 
( 1 ) use and counting of chemically trated ballot 

papers numbering about a lac or more in 

favour of respondent No. 1; 
( 2 ) bribery and payment of cash money for secur 

Ing votes (Issue No. 1 ); 
( 3 ) appeal to voters on the ground of religion ; 

caste ; community and language ( Issue No. 2 ) ; 
( 4 ) Appeal to voters in the name of cow and calf 

as a religious symbol (Issue No . 3.); 
( 5 ) obtaining or procuring assistance of gazetted 

officers , members of the Armed forces of the 
Union ; members of the Police Force and Excisę 

Officers ( Issue No. 4 ) ; 
( 6 ) Incurring or authorising expenditure in excess 

of section 77 (Issues Nos . 5 and 6 ) and 
( 7 ) attacking the personal character or conduct of 

the petitioner ( Issue No. 7 ). 
None of these Issues has been proved . 

The result is that the election petition is dismissed 
In view of the fact that the petition has not gone into 
full trial, and no evidence has been recorded , the order 
as to costs will be that the petitioner , will pay Rs. 500 / 
as costs to respondent No . 1 . 

Intimation of this decision t obe sent by the office 
as required under section 103 of the Act.

of which was upon the petitioner it has to be held that 
the petitirner has failed to substantiate any of those 
Issues which must be decided against him for this very 
reason . It is not necessary to call upon respondent No . 
I to furnish any evidence on these Issues . 

The result , therefore, is that Issues Nos. 1 to 7 are 
decided against the petitioner .

एम . न . 1120. - - भावश्यक वन अधिनियम , 1955 
( 185547 10) की धारा द्वारा प्रदान गतियों का प्रयोग करते

4. अधोहस्ताक्षरी ने मामले को भली भांति पांच कर ली है 
पौर इस परिणाम पर पहुंचा है कि सर्वधी सन्दीप बल्ब मै००, 
देहरादून का कारखाना बन्द हो गया है और लाइसेंस जिस उद्देश्य 
की पूर्ति के लिए जारी किया गया था , उसे पूरा नहीं करेगा ।

( 309 ) / 1989/ कान , दिनांक 15 अक्टूबर, 1970 यह पूछो

को क्यों न रद्द कर दिया जा चाहिए और वह इस प्राधार पर कि 
उनके कारखाने में कार्य प्रार अनुरक्षण के लिए कोई सुविधा 
नहीं थी , इसलिए जिस उद्देश्य की पूर्ति के लिए लाइसेंस जारी 
किया गया था उसे पूरा नहीं करेगा ।

में कार्य और अनुरक्षण की सुविधा थी ।

अवधि के लिए प्रायात व्यापार नियंत्रण नीति पुस्तक 
वा० - 1 के परिशिष्ट 32 में उल्लिखित स्वीकृत किस्म के फालतू 
पूजों के प्रायात के लिए 5, 00, 000 रु० का एक लाइसेंस 
स० पी / एस / 1650670 दिनांक 28 - 6 - 1969 स्वीकृति 
किया गया था । 

सत्पश्चात् उन्हें एक कारण बताओ सूचना सं० इन्फ 2 
( 65 )/ 1971 कान० दिनांक 5 नवम्बर, 1971 यह पूछते 
हुए जारी की गई थी कि उक्त सूचना की प्राप्त के 15 दिनों 
के भीतर कारण बताए कि उनके नाम में जारी किए गए 
उपर्युक्त लाइसेंस को क्यों रद्द कर दिया जाना चाहिए और वह 
इस प्राधार पर कि उनका कोई भी कारखाना विद्यमान नहीं था 
पोर इसलिए लाइसेंस मिथ्यनिरुपण द्वारा प्राप्त किया गया था । 

उपर्यक्त फारण बतायो सूचना डाक प्राधिकारियों द्वारा इस 
टिप्पणी के साथ बिना बाट वापिस लौटा दी गई थी कि दिए गए

प्रतियों के लिए इस आधार पर आवेदन किया है कि मल प्रतियां 

खो गई हैं । प्रागे यह बताया गया है कि मूल लाइसेंस किसी भी 
सीमाशुल्क प्राधिकारी के पास पंजाकट नहीं करवाया गया था और 
साइसेंस तथा रिहाई मादेश के पूरे मूल्य का उपयोग नहीं किया 
गया था । 

अपने तक के समर्थन में प्रावक ने यह बताते हुए शपथ पत्र 
दाखिल किया है कि उपर्युक्त लाइसेंस और रिहाई आदेश की मूल 
प्रतियां ( दोनों सीमाशुल्क कार्य संबंधी और मद्रा विनिमय नियंत्रण 
प्रतियां ) खो गई हैं और निदेश दिया जाता है कि आवेदक को 
उन्हीं की तीन अनुलिपि प्रतियां जारी की जानी चाहिए । उपर्यक्त 
लाइसेंस और रिहाई प्रादेश की मल सीमा - शुल्क कार्य संबंधी और 
मुद्रा विनिमय नियंत्रण प्रतियां एतदद्वाग रद्द की जाती हैं ।

का० प्रा० 1163 :---- यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय 
है कि इससे उपाब अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स 
देहरी रोहतास लाइट रेलये कम्पनी, लिमिटेड, डालमिया नगर , 
के प्रबन्ध से सम्बस नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक 
औद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है । 


और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उस्त विवाद को म्यानिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है :

S .O . 1190. - Whereas Messrs Glad Stone Lyall and 
Company Limited , 4 , Fairlie Place, Calcutta - l (here 
inafter referred to as the said establishment) hus 
applied for exemption under clause ( a ) of sub - section 
( 1 ) of section 17 of the Employees Provident Funds 
and Family Dension Fund Act, 1952 ( 19 of 1952 ) ; 

And whereas in the opinion of the Central Govern 
ment. the rules of the provident fund of the said 
establishment with respect to the rates of contribution 
are not less favourable to the employees therein than 
those specified in section 6 of the said Act, and the 
employees are also in enjoyment of other provident 
fund benefits which on the whole are not less favour 
able to the employees than the benents provided under 
the said Act or under the Employees Provident Funds 
Scheme, 1952 ( hereinafter referred to as the said 
Scheme) in relation to the employees in any othe : 
establishment of a similar character; 

Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred 
by clause ( a ) of sub- section ( 1 ) of section 17 of the 
said Act , and subject to the conditions specified in the 
Schedule annexed hereto , the Central Government 
hereby exempts the said establishment from the opera 
tion of all the provisions of the said Scheme and in 
rursuance of sub - section ( 3 ) of the said section 17, the 
Central Government hereby directs that -- 
( a ) the employer in relation to the said establish 

ment shall pay within Afteen days of the 
close of the month to the Employees Provident 
Fund , inspection charges at the rate of 0 .09 
per cent ( zero point zero nine per cent) of the 
pay (basic wages , dearness allowance , retaining 
allowance, if any, and cash value of fool 
concession Admissihle thereon ) for the time 
being payable to the employers of the said 
establishment who would have become meni 
bers under the said Scheme hut for this 

exemption ; 
( b ) the said employer shall invest the provident 

fund contributions In acordance with the direr 
tions issued by the Central Government from 
time to time.

___ 7. स्थापन अपनी भविष्य निधि का संपरीक्षित तुलन-पत्र हर 
वर्ष प्रादेशिक केन्द्रीय भविष्य निधि आयुक्त को वर्षान्त के 
तीन मास के भीतर भेजेगा । 

8 भविष्य निधि नियमों में कोई भी संशोधन केन्द्रीय भविष्य 
निधि आयुक्त के पूर्व अनुमोदन के बिना नहीं किया जाएगा । जहां 
किसी संशोधन से कर्मचारियों के हितों पर प्रतिकूल प्रभाव पड़ना 
संभाव्य हो वहां प्रादेशिक केन्द्रीय भविष्य निधि आयुक्त , अपना 
अनुमोदन देने से पूर्व , कर्मचारियों को अपना दृष्टिकोण स्पष्ट करने 
का युक्तियुक्त अवसर देगा ।

अनुसूची 
1. नियोजक प्रादेशिक भविष्य निधि प्रायुक्त को वे विवरणिय 
भेजेगा जिन्हें केन्द्रीय सरकार समय - समय पर विहित करे । 

2. नियोजक प्रत्येक कर्मचारी को वार्षिक लेखा-विवरण या 
पास बुक भेजेगा । 

3. निधि के प्रशासन , जिसमें लेखाओं का बनाए रखना , 
लेखानों और विवरणियों का भेजा जाना , संचयों का अन्तरण , 
निरीक्षण -प्रभारों आदि का संदाय सम्मिलित हैं , मैं अन्सर्वलित 
सभी व्ययों का वहन नियोजक द्वारा किया जाएगा । 
____ 4. नियोजक समुचित सरकार द्वारा अनुमोदित निधि के 
नियमों की एक प्रति स्थापन के सूचना -पट्ट पर प्रदर्शित करेगा 

और जब कभी उनमें संशोधन किया जाएगा तब कर्मचारियों की 
बहुसंख्या की भाषा में उसकी मुख्य मुख्य बातों का अनुवाद भी प्रदर्शित 
करेगा । 

5. यदि कोई ऐसा कर्मचारी , जो कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि 
( कानूनी निधि ) या छूट प्राप्त किसी अन्य स्थापन की भविष्य 
निधि का पहले ही से सदस्य है , उसके स्थापन में नियोजित होता है 
तो नियोजक स्थापन की निधि के सदस्य के रूप में उसका नाम तुरन्त 
ही दर्ज करेगा और ऐसे कर्मचारी की बाबत पिछले संचयों को स्वीकार 
करके उन्हें उसके खाते में जमा करेगा ।

( i) fallure to submit returns In timc; 
( 11) delay in payment of inspection charges; 
( III ) fallure to transfer provident fund money to 

the Board of trustees, 
And whereas the said Establishment by reason of 
the commission of the aforesaid defaults have failed

exemption was granted to the Establishment ; 

And whereas the Central Government is satisfied 
that the failure to comply with the conditions has con 
tinued without any sufficient cause inspite of reason 
able opportunity having being given to the said Estab 
lishment to comply with those conditions; 

Now , therefore, in cxercise of the powers conferred 
hy sub - section ( 4 ) of section 17 of the said Act, the 
Central Government hereby cancels, with immediate 
effect , the exemption granted to the sald Establishment .

की धारा 17 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( क ) के अधीन छूट देने के 
लिए आवेदन किया है । 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय में अभिदाय की दरों की 
बाबत उक्त स्थापन के भविष्य निधि नियम उसके कर्मचारियों के 
लिए उन नियमों से कम अनुकूल नहीं हैं जो उक्त अधिनियम की 
धारा 6 में विनिर्दिष्ट हैं , और कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि की अन्य 
प्रसुविधाएं भी पा रहे हैं जो कर्मचारियों के लिए कुल मिला कर 
उन प्रसुविधाओं से कम अनुकूल नहीं हैं , जो , उसी प्रकार के किसी 
अन्य स्थापन के कर्मचारियों के सम्बन्ध में , उक्त अधिनियम के अधीन 

और कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि स्कीम , 1952 ( जिसे इसमें इसके 
पश्चात् उक्त स्कीम कहा गया है ) के अधीन दी जाती है ; 

प्रत : अब, उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 17 की उपधारा ( 1 ) 
के खण्ड ( क ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए और इससे 
उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट शर्तों के अधीन रहते हए , केन्द्रीय 
सरकार उक्त स्थापन को उक्त स्कीम के सभी उपबन्धों के प्रवर्तन 


Now , therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred! 
by clause ( a ) of sub -section ( 1 ) of section 17 of the 
said Act, and subject to the conditions specified in the

bereby exempts the said establishment from the opera

8. भविष्य निधि नियमों में कोई भी संशोधन केन्द्रीय 
भविष्य निधि आयुक्त के पूर्व अनुमोदन के बिना नहीं किया जाएगा । 
जहां किसी संशोधन से कर्मचारियों के हितों पर प्रतिकूल प्रभाव 
पड़ना संभाव्य हो वहां केन्द्रीय भविष्य निधि आयुक्त अपना अनु 
मोदन देने से पूर्व कर्मचारियों को अपना दृष्टिकोण स्पष्ट करने 
का युक्तियुक्त अवसर देगा । 


[ सं० एस - 35014 ( 4 ) / 71- पी०एफ० - 2 ] 


से एतद्वारा छुट देती है और उक्त धारा 17 की उपधारा ( 3 ) के 
अनुसरण में केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा निदेश देती है कि - - 

क ) उक्त स्थापन से सम्बद्ध नियोजक उक्त स्थापन के 

उन कर्मचारियों को , जो , यदि यह छूट न दी गई 
होती तो , उक्त स्कीम के अधीन सदस्य हो गए 
होने , तत्समय देय वेतन के ( प्राधारिक मजदूरी , 
महंगाई भत्ता, प्रतिधारण भत्ता , यदि कोई हो , और 
उस पर अनुज्ञेय खाद्य रियायत का नकद मूल्य ) 
0 . 09 ( शून्य दशमलव शून्य नौ ) प्रतिशत की 
दर से निरीक्षण- प्रभार मासान्त के पन्द्रह दिन के 
भीतर कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि को देगा ; 
उक्त नियोजक भविष्य निधि अभिदायों को , केन्द्रीय 
सरकार द्वारा समय - समय पर निकाले गए निदेशों 
के अनुसार, विनिहित करेगा । 

अनुसूची 
1. नियोजक प्रादेशिक भविष्य निधि आयुक्त को वे विवरणियां 
भेजेगा जिन्हें केन्द्रीय सरकार समय समय पर विहित करे । 

2. नियोजक प्रत्येक कर्मचारी को वार्षिक लेखा विवरण या 
पास बुक भेजेगा । 

3. निधि के प्रणागन , जिसमें लेखाओं का बनाए रखना, लेखाओं 
और विवरणियों का भेजा जाना ; संचयों का अन्तरण , निरीक्षण 
प्रभारों आदि का संदाय सम्मिलित हैं , में अन्तर्वलित सभी व्ययों का 
वहन नियोजक द्वारा किया जाएगा । 

4. नियोजक समुचित सरकार द्वारा अनुमोदित निधि के 
नियमों की एक प्रति स्थापन के सूचना -पट्ट पर प्रदर्शित करेगा और 
जब कभी उनमें संशोधन किया जाएगा तब कर्मचारियों की बहुसंख्या 
की भाषा में उसकी मुख्य -मुख्य बातों का अनुवाद भी प्रदर्शित करेगा । 

5. यदि कोई ऐसा कर्मचारी , जो कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि 
( कानूनी निधि ) या छूट-प्राप्त किसी अन्य स्थापन की भविष्य निधि 
का पहले ही से सदस्य है, उसके स्थापन में नियोजित होता है तो 
नियोजक स्थापन की निधि के सदस्य के रूप में उसका नाम तुरन्त ही 
दर्ज करेगा और ऐसे कर्मचारी की बाबत उसके पिछले संचयों को 
स्वीकार करके उन्हें उसके खाते में जमा करेगा । 

6. यदि उस वर्ग के स्थापनों के लिए , जिसमें नियोजक का 
स्थापन प्राता है , भविष्य निधि के अभिदायों की दर कर्मचारी 
भविष्य निधि और परिवार पेंशन निधि अधिनियम , 1952 के 
अधीन बढ़ा दी जाए तो नियोजक भविष्य निधि के अभिदायों की 
दर समुचित रूप में बढ़ा देगा ताकि स्थापन की भविष्य निधि स्कीम 
के अधीन की प्रसुविधाएं उन प्रसुविधाओं से कम अनुकूल न हो जाएं 
जिनकी व्यवस्था कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि और परिवार पेंगन निधि 
अधिनियम , 1952 के अधीन है । 

7. स्थापन अपनी भविप्य निधि का संपरीक्षित तुलन 
पत्र हर वर्ष प्रादेशिक भविष्य निधि आयुक्त को वर्षान्त के तीन 
मास के भीतर भेजेगा ।

का० प्रा० 1193.-- - यतः मैसर्स दि न्यू इण्डिया एशोरेंस 
कम्पनी लिमिटेड, 87 एम० जी० रोड , कोर्ट , बम्बई - 1 (जिसे

भविष्य निधि अधिनियम 1952 ( 1952 का 19 ) (जिसे 
इसमें इसके पश्चात् उक्त अधिनियम कहा गया है ) की धारा 17 
की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( क ) के अधीन छूट देने के लिए आवेदन 
किया है । 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय में अभिदाय की दरों 
की बाबत उक्त स्थापन के भविष्य निधि नियम उसके कर्मचारियों 
के लिए उन नियमों से कम अनुकूल नहीं हैं जो उम्त अधिनियम की 
धारा 6 में विनिर्दिष्ट हैं , और कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि की अन्य 
प्रसुविधाएं भी पा रहे है जो कर्मचारियों के लिए कुल मिलाकर 
उन प्रसुविधाओं से कम अनुकूल नहीं , जो , उसी प्रकार के किसी 
अन्य स्थापन के कर्मचारियो के सम्बन्ध में , उक्त अधिनियम के 
अधीन और कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि स्कीम , 1952 (जिसे इसमें 
इसके पश्चात् उक्त स्वीम कहा गया है ) के अधीन दी जाती 
जर ; 


अनसूची 
1. नियोजक प्रादेशिक केन्द्रीय भविष्य निधि प्रायुक्त को 
यविवरणियां भेजेगा जिन्हें केन्द्रीय सरकार समय - समय पर विहित 
करे । . 

2. नियोजक प्रत्यक कर्मचारी को वार्षिक लेखा -विवरण 
या पास बुक भेजेगा । 

3. निधि के प्रशासन , जिसमें लेखाओं का बनाए रखना , 
लेखाओं और विवरणियों का भेजा जाना , संचयो का अन्तरण , 
निरीक्षण -प्रभारों आदि का दाय सम्मिलित है, में अन्तलिस 
सभी व्ययों का वहन नियोजक द्वारा किया जाएगा । 

4. नियोजक समचित सरकार द्वारा अनमोदित निधि के 
नियमों की एक प्रति स्थापन के सूचना -पट्ट पर प्रदर्शित करेगा 
और जब कभी उनमें संशोधन किया जाएगा तब कर्मचारियों की 
बहसंख्या की भाषा में उसकी मुख्य मुख्य बातों का अनुवाद भी 
प्रदर्शित करेगा । 

5. यदि कोई ऐसा कर्मचारी , जो कर्मचारी भविष्यविधि 
( काननी निधि ) या छुट- प्राप्त किसी अन्य स्थापन की भविष्य 
निधि का पहले ही से सदस्य है, उसके स्थापन में नियोजित 
होता है तो नियोजक स्थापन की निधि के रूप में उसका नाम तुरन्त 
ही दर्ज करेगा और ऐसे कर्मचारी की बाबत उसके पिछले संचयों 
को स्वीकार करके उन्हें उसके खाते में जमा करेगा । 


6 . यदि उस वर्ग के स्थापनों के लिए, जिसमें नियोजक का 
स्थापन प्राता है, भविष्य निधि के अभिदायों की दर कर्मचारी 
भविष्य निधि और कुटम्ब पेंशन निधि अधिनियम , 1952 के 
अधीन बढ़ा दी जाए , तो नियोजक भविष्य निधि के अभिदायों की 
दर समचित रूप से बढ़ा देगा ताकि स्थापन की भविष्य निधि स्कीम 
के अधीन की प्रसुविधाएं उन प्रसुविधाओं से कम अनुकल न हो जाएं 
जिनकी व्यवस्था कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि अधिनियम और कुटम्ब 
पेंशन निधि , 1952 के अधीन है । 


अतः, अब , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 17 क उपधारा ( 1 ) 
के खण्ड ( क ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियो का प्रयोग करते हई और 
इससे उपाबव अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट शर्तों के अधीन रहते हए , 
केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त स्थापन को उक्त स्कीम के सभी उपबन्धों के 
प्रवर्तन से एतद्द्वारा छूट देती है और उक्त धारा 17 की उपधारा 
( 3 ) के अनुसरण में केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा निदेश देती है 


कि 


( क ) उक्त स्थापन से सम्बद्ध नियोजक उक्त स्थापन के 

उन कर्मचारियों को , जो यदि छूट न दी गई होती 
तो , उक्त स्कीम के अधीन सदस्य हो गए होते , 


7. स्थापन अपनी भविष्य निधि का संपरीक्षित तुलनपत्र

S . O . 1195 . - Whereas the Central Goverinent was 
satisfied that, 

1. Kathiawar Ginning Factory & O11 Mill . 
2. T he Kundla Press & Oil Mill P . Ltd . 
3 . Saranjam Vibhag Khadi Karyalaya . 
4 . Rajni Oil Mill. 

5 . Savarakundla Electric Supply Co. Ltd. 
were situated in Savarkundla area which was a gparse 
area ( that is , an area whose insurable population was 
less than 500 ) in the district of Bhavnagar in the State 
of Gujarat : 

And , whereas by virtue of their location in a sparse 
area, the aforesaid factories were granted exemption 
from the payment of the employer s special contribu 
tion under section 73F of the Employees State Insur 
ance Act , 1948 ( 34 of 1948 ) until enforcement of the 
provisions of Chapter V of the said Act in that area 
by the Central Government in the notification of the 
Government of India in the late Ministry of Labour 
and Employment No . S . O . 137, dated the 9th January , 
1962: 

And , whereas the Central Government is satisfled 
that the insurable population of the Savarkundla area 
in the district of Bhavnagar in the State of Gujarat 
has now exceeded 500 , and it is no longer a sparse 
area ; 


अनुसूची 
1. नियोजक प्रादेशिक भविष्य निधि प्रायुक्त को वे विवरणियां 
भेजेगा जिन्हें केन्द्रीय सरकार समय - समय पर विहित करे । 
___ 2. नियोजक प्रत्येक कर्मचारी को वार्षिक लेखा-विवरण 
या पास बुक भेजेगा । 

3. निधि के प्रशासन , जिसमें लेखाओं का बनाए रखना , 
रेखाओं और विवरणियों का भेजा जाना , संचयों का अन्तरण, निरी 
क्षण-प्रभारों आदि का संदाय सम्मिलित है , में अन्तवलित सभी 
व्ययों का वहन नियोजक द्वारा किया जाएगा । 
____ 4. नियोजक समुचित सरकार द्वारा अनुमोदित निधि के 
नियमों की एक प्रति स्थापन के सूचना-पद पर प्रदर्शित करेगा 

और जब कभी उनमें संशोधन किया जाएगा तब कर्मचारियों की 
बहुसंख्या की भाषा ; उसकी मुख्य-मुख्य बातों का अनुवाद भी 
प्रदर्शित करेगा । 

5. यदि कोई ऐसा कर्मचारी, जो कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि के 
( कानूनी निधि ) या छूट प्राप ,किसी अन्य स्थापन की भविष्य 
निधि का पहले ही से सदस्य , है, उसके स्थापन में नियोजित होता है 
तो नियोजक स्थापना की निधि के सदस्य के रूप में उसका नामतुरंत 
ही दर्ज करेंगा और कर्मचारी की बाबत उम पिछले संचयों को 
स्वीकार करके उन्हें उसक खाते में जमा करेगा । 

___ 6. यदि उस वर्ग के स्थापनों के लिए, जिसमें नियोजक का 
स्थापन पाता हैं , भविष्य निधि के अभिदायों की दर कर्मचारी 
भविष्य निधि और कुटुम्ब पैशन निधि अधिनियम , 1952 के 
अधीन बढ़ा दी जाए तो नियोजक भविष्य निधि के अभिवायों की 
दर समुचित रूप से बढ़ा देगा ताकि स्थापन की भविष्य निधि 
स्कीम के अधीन की प्रसुविधाएं उन प्रसुविधानों से कम अनुकूल 
न हो जाएं जिनकी व्यवस्था कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि और कुटुम्ब 
पेंशन निधि अधिनियम , 1952 के अधीन है । 


Now , therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred 
by section 73F of the Employees State Insurance Act , 
1948 ( 34 of 1948 ), the Central Government hereby 
makes the following further amendment in the said 
notiication, namely : - - 
In the Schedule to the said notification , against 

Serial No . 5 . the entry " Savarkundla " in 
column 3 and the corresponding entries in 
columns 4 and shall he omitted

S . O . 1226. -.- whereas the Central Government being 
satisfied that the public interest so required , had de 
clared by a notification made in pursuance of the pro 
visions of the proviso to sub - clause ( vi) of clause ( n )

3830 , dated the 13th September, 1971 ] the lead mining 
industry to be a public utility service for the purposes 
of the said Act for a period of six months from the 25th 
September , 1971 ; 

And whereas the Central Government is of opinion 
that the public interest requires the extension of the 
said period by a further period of six months; 

Now , therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred 
by the proviso to sub - clause ( vi ) of clause ( n ) of 
section 2 of the Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 ( 14 of 
1947 ) , the Central Government hereby declares the 
said industry to be a public utility service for the 
purposes of the said Act for a further period of six 
months from the 25th March , 1972 .

By Order No. 6 /40 / 70 - LRII, dated 4th March , 1971 , 
the Government of India , in the Ministry of Labour, 
Employment and Rehabilitation (Department of Lab 
our and Employment ) , referred the following indus 
trial dispute existing between the employers in rela 
tion to the management of Bankola Colliefy and their 
workmen , to this Tribunal, for adjudication , namcly : 
" Whether the management of Bankola Colliery of 

Messrs Burrakur Coal Company Limited , Post 
Office Ukhra, District Burdwan was justifled 
in stopping from work Saryashri Sunit Das, 
Narayan Das , Bharat Roy , Tilok Gope , Jakir 
Hosen , Govind Nandi, Moheswar Jha, Clean 
ing Mazdoors from 2nd June, 1970 and Shri 
Ajit Roy . Cleaning Mazdoor from the 29th 
May , 1970 ? If not, to what relief these work 
men are entitled ?" 


4 , This Tribunal on 10th April, 1972 passed the 
following orders and notified both the parties accord 
ingly : 
“ Since the Memorandum of settlement filed 

requires certain clarification , let the matter 
be fixed again on 28th April, 1972 for record 
ing settlement or for some other order / orders 

as this Tribunal woull deem fit and proper ." 
Unfortunately the union has not turned up in spite of 
notice and the nignagement has turned up. As the 
Union does not appear, this tribunal considers that 
there is no dispute over the matter referred to it for 
adjudication . So , I render an award to the effect that 
there is " no dispute " over the inalter referred to adju 
dication by this Tribunal. 


2 . The management and the union have come to a 
compromise over the matters referred to for adjudi 
cation and have filed a joint memorandum of terms of 
compromise for recording and passing an award thereof 
and have prayed for including the terms of the memo 
randum of compromise in the award . I have consi 
dered the terms of compromise as recorded in the 
memorandum and heard the representatives of the 
parties . The terms are fair , just and equitable and 
are beneficial to the interest of tlic workmen who 
were engaged by the contractors when the dispute 
ulose but are now to be included in the list of subs 
titute workers as maintained by the colliery under 
the certified Standing orders and would thereby have 
a chance of getting appointment directly in the coll 
j.ery . Hence the memorandum of compromise be re 
corded and an award be rendered thereon making 
such memorandum a part of the award , 


This is my award . 


The reference is accordingly disposed of.

2 . Under paragraph 3 of the settlement it is avreed

by this Tribunal in full settlement of the demand in 
this respect. The Company is prepared to pay any 
amount whereas the union wants atleast a payment 
of one montla and 22 days , I direct the concern that it 
shall under this clause make an ex -gratia payment of 
twenty two days wages. This disposes of the reference.

4 . Let me consider if there are any collective disputes 
belween the employers on the one hand and their 
employees on the other . The present iwo dispues 
bave peen raised by the union known as the “ Mine

numper , have, as stated before , been raised by the 
Mine Karmachari Sangh , which was not functioning

11 . The story given by W . W . 1 as to how the members

Arst time, he says, the union started enrolling memhers

14 . Even if the receipts and the Register of member 
ship are taken to be genuine, even then there is nothing 
on record to show that the present two disputes have 
been sponsored by the members of the union working 
in the Badjna Colliery or by the union itsell . The law 
on this point has thus been laid down by the Supreme 
Court in workmen of Dharampal Prem Chand and 
Dharampal Premchand , (1965 ) I L . L . J . 668 : " In every 
case where industrial adjudication has to decide whether 
a reference in regard to the dismissal of an Industrial 
employee is validly made or not, it would always be 
necessary to enquire whether the union which has 
sponsored the case can fairly claim a representative 
character in such a way that its support to the cause 
would make the dispute an industrial dispute . . . . . . . . . 
( In ) some cases the union of workmen working in one 
industry may be competent to raise a dispute about 
the wrongful dismissal of an employee engaged in an 
establishment belonging to the same industry where 
workmen in such an establishment have no union of 
their own , and an appreciable number of such work 
men had joined such other union before their dis 
missal." In the instant cases the Mine Karmachari 
Sangh , a general union appears to have espoused the 
cause of the four workmen stopped from working . The 
Colliery Mazdoor Sangh . the recognised union , has 
heen functioning in the Badina Colllery for a long time. 
The Colliery Mazdoor Sangh also appears to be a 
general union . So the workmen of the Badina Colllery 
have no exclusive union of their own . The Mine 
Karmachari Sangh is no douht competent to espouse 
the cause of the workmen stopped from work by the 
management of Badina Colliery, but before doing so 
the union must establish that an appreciable number 
of the workmen of the Badina Colliery had joined the 
Mine Karmachari Sangh hefore the workmen concerned 
had been stopped from work . But all the four work 
men , involved in the two references were stopped from 
work before an appreciable number of the workmen 
of the Badina Colliery joined the Mine Karmachari 
Sangh . Again , a question may pertinently be asked 
whether 349 out of more than 1500 workmen may be 
regarded as an appreciable number. W . W . 1 has said 
That 1600 to 1700 workmen work in the Bndjna Collierv , 
If that be co , 349 cannot be regarded as an appreciable 
number . Then again , there is nothing on record to 
show that even the Mine Karmachari Sangh has really 
espnused the cause of the four workmen stopped from 
vork . NO resolution of the union has been produced 
to indicato that the union has taken up the cause of 
the four workinen or that the union has authorised the 
General Secretary to raise industrial disputes on behalf 
of the concerned workmen . 


15 . In Bombay Union of Journalists and The Hindu . 
Pinmhay , 1961 ( 2 ) n . 436 the resolution Authorising 
the poneral union to espouse the couse of the dis 
missed workman was found to be a fabrication . The 
Suorcme Court, therefore , came to the conclusion that 
the union was not competent to espouse the cause of 
the dismissed worlmen . In the instant two cases there 
is no resnlution whatsoever authorising the union to 
take up the cause of the workmen stopped from work 
Ing.

1965 ( 1 ) L .LJ. 0 .95 , the dispute of an individual 
moriter was taken up and referred for adjudication 
of thn instance of a general union of which some of 
tho follow worlmen in the establishment, were members .

observed the Madras High Court , that they participated 
in or orted together and arrived at an understanding hy 
A resolution or by other mearg and collectively sup 
torted on the date of the reference the demand or 
cause of the individual worker . This principle holds 
good in the case of a collective dispute even after the

17 . In Murugappa Mudaliar , Rathina Mudaliar & Sons 
and Raju Mudaliar & Others 1965 ( I) L . L . J . 489 the 
following test was laid down for ascertaining when 
an individual dispute becomes a collective dispute : 
" From these decisions it is clear that an individual 
dispute can become an Industrial dispute only if the 
grievance of an employee is taken up by his fellow 
workers or a substantial section of them working under 
the game employer. " The fart that his grievance has 
been taken up by a union , consigting of workmen having 
no interest in his dispute. cannot convert that disoute 
Into an Industrial dispute . In the instant cases there 
is nothing on record to show that the griuvance of the 
four employees stopped from work has heen taken 
110 by their fellow workers or a substantial section of 
them working under the same employer . Even assum 
ing that the Mine Karmachart Şangh , has raised the 
disputes on behalf of the aggrieved workmen , it is not 
known how this union espoused the cause of the ag 
grieved workmen whether by a resolution or by any 
other means. Il by a resolution it is not known how 
many of the fellow workmen participated in the said 
rogolution . This union cannot ask for reference unless 
It is requested to do go by such of its members as are 
employed in the Badjna Colliery . As the validity of 
the two references has been challenged by the manage 
ment, it was the duty of the union to supply materials 
to satisfy the Tribunal that the references have been 
valldly made in respect of collective disputes . But no 
such materials have been supplied by the union . 


18 . The foregoing discussion makes it clear that each 
one of the two references relate to an individual, as 
opposed to a collective dispute . But the references 
purport to be in respective of collective disputes, that 
is to say , in respect of disputes between the workmen 
of the Badina Collierv and their emolovers. As the 
existence of any collertive dignute between the 
emplovers and their workmen has not been estahlished 
in either of the twn references . the only award that 
can he given in each of these two roferences is of A 
no dispute Award , and I award accordingly in each 
one of the two references heard together .

8 . Ext. W9 series were filed by the union just to

9 . I have no reason to disbelieve M . W . 1 who says 
in his examination in chief that he was employed on 
a temporary basis . The relevant portion of his de 
position is as follows: " He ( the concerned workman ) 
worked in the colliery from 17th February, 1971 to 
19th March , 1971. I appointed this workman in place 
of permanent incumbent Shri Dibakar Mukherjec, 
Can Lamp Maintenance Clerk who went on leave from 
17th February , 1971 for onc month . I did not give 
any appointment letter . I made it clear to him that 
this is a purely temporary job and I also told him 
that he would have to vacate when the permanent 
incumbent would come. The permanent incumbent 
Dibakar Mukhrrjen joined his duty on 20th March , 
1971 . Hence from that date the services of Shri Ram 
Kishun Prasad were terminated " . There is no serious 
cross - examination on this point. 

10 . The workman concerned in his deposition no 
doubt has said that he was appointed on a permanent 
hasis but he has not been able to produce any evidence 
in support of that statement. He has further stated in 
his examination - in - chief that he was not apnointed in 
place of Shri Dibakar Mukherjee ; but from Ext. MI 
it is clear that ho was appointed in the leave vacancy 
of Dlbakar Mukherjee . Ext . W4 does not show that 
the concerned workman was employed on a perma 
nent basis. 

11 . It is argued by Shri S . P . Singh that the con 
cerned workman must be regarded as a permanent 
workman as he was employed in a post appertaining 
to one of the permanent operations of the colliery . I 
cannot accept this contention . Even a temporary 
workman can be appointed for doing work of a per 
manent nature . The nature of the work performed by 
the workman cannot indicate whether he is permanent 
or temporary . 

12 . Shri Singh argues that no appointment can be 
made as is not provided for in the standing orders . 
Clause 3 of the standing orders classifies workmen 
into : ( i ) Permanent ; ( ii ) Probationers ; ( ili ) Badlis or 
Substitutes ; ( iv ) Temporary and ( v ) Apprentices . 
The employer has not described the workman concer 
ned as a badii. Shri Singh argues that thn roncerned 
workman is neither a probationer nor a badli nor a 
temporary workman nor an Apprentice . Ho is also 
not a temporary workman because according to the 
definition in clause 3 of the standing orders a fem 
porary workman is one who is engaged for a specifled 
temnorary work . Shri Singh argues that the concerned 
workman was not engaged for any temporary work . 
According to Shri Singh as he is neither a probationer 
nor a hadli nor temporary nor an apprentice he must 
bn Tagarded as a permanent workman . This artiment 
also I am unable to accept. The standing orders no 
where says that a temporary appointment cannot be 
made in respect of a permanent work . If any work 
man is anpinted temporarily in respect of a permanent 
work and he accepts the appointment as a temporary 
workman , ly must be regarded as a temporary work 
man . In this connection reference may be made to 
the capision of the Supreme Court in Rohtas Indus 
trios Ltd And Brijandan Pandey and other 1956 ( II ) 
I LJP. 444 . In that case the company had a number 
of factories at Dalmianngar including a cement factory, 
nower -house, pulp mil), paper factory , chemical fac . 
tory for the manufacture of certain acids and an 
Asbestos cement factory . The company had a number 
of temporary employees who were engaged tempo 
rarily in connection with certain erection works for 


6 . In as much as the locus -standt of the union bas 
been challenged by the employer the onus lies on the 
union to establish its authority to raise an industrial 
dispute on behalf of the concerned workman . No such 
evidence has been adduced by the union before this 
Tribunal; in other words, the union has falled to dis 
charge the onus of proving that it was dulv authorised 
by the workmen of the coiliery to raise this dispute . 
That being the position the present dispute cannot be 
regarded as a collertive dispute ; at best it may be 
treated as an individual dispute under gection 2A of the 
Inrlustrial Disputes Act. The present dispute has been 
referred to this Tribunal as a collective dispute and has 
pot to be decided on that footing. From what has heen 
stated above it is clear that the uninn is not entitled to 
represent the workman conrerned . The present disrutn 
thereform Cannot be entertained by this Tribunal. The 
reterence is liable to le nirmiscort on that ground . My 
conclusion on this point is based on the followin 
rases : ( 1 ) Workmen of Dharampal Premchand And 
Dharampal Premchand . 1965 ( I ) L . L . J . 6R8 ; ( 2 ) Nella ! 
Cotton Mills And Labour Court , Madurai, 1965 (11) 
T .LJ, 98 ; ( 3 ) Murupanna Mudaliar Rathina Mudaliar & 
Song And Raju Mudaliar, 1965 ( T) L . L . J . 489 . 


7 . Apart from what has been stated above, on merits

the extension and enlargement of those factories. The 
terms of employment of these employees were em 
bodied in a temporary appointment from which was 
signed by the employees as well as the manageinment. 
The work for which they were recruited was over some 
time before and after such work they were engaged 
in a permanent work in production department. The 
employer thereafter applied to the Labour Appallate 
Tribunal for permission to dismiss them . The permission 
was refused by the Tribunal but on appeal was given 
by the Supreme Court for the following reason : 
“ Under their terms of employment, temporary em 
ploees could be moved from one work to the other 
And the more circumstance that they were employed 
in a production department for some time, even if 
true , did not make them permanent employees ; nor 
did the circumstance that they enjoyed some of the 
benefits of permanent employees make them perma 
nent" . On the same principles it may be said that the 
concerned workman employed on a temporary basis 
must be regarded as a temporary workman even though 
he was employed for the purpose of performing some 
permanent work in the colliery . 

13 . It is therefore clear from what has been stated 
above that the action of the management of Fast, 
Johapati Colllery in terminating the services of Shri 
Ram Kishun Prasad , Cap Lamp Maintenance Clerk with 
effect from 19th March , 1971 is justified and , thrrefore , 
he is not entitled to any relief . My award is as 
follows: As the present dispute has not bern raised hy 
a substantial number of workmen working in the 
colliery it cannot be regarded as a collective dispute 
and it cannot be decided as such . In other words no 
award can be given as there is no dispute . My further 
award is that even on merits the action of the 
management in terminating the services of Shri Ram 
Kishun Prasad is justified and that he is not entitled 
to any relief .

सन्स , जिप्सम ठेकेदार, भादवासी के प्रबंध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और 
उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

पौर यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 


का० प्रा० 1235 . - - यत :, केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है 
कि इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स 
श्रोरियन्टल कोल कम्पनी लिमिटेड की बादजना कोलियरी, डाकघर 
निसचिट्टी, जिला धनबाद के प्रवन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और 
उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 


और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद कोन्यायन 
के लिये निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 


प्रत : अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 7 - क और धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के 
खंड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , केन्द्रीय सरकार 
एतदद्वारा एक प्रोद्योगिक प्रधिकरण गठित करती है जिसके पीठा 
सीन अधिकारी श्री गोपाल नारायण शर्मा होंगे , जिनका मुख्यालय 
अयपुर होगा और उक्त विवाद को उक्त प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण को 
न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 


अब , यत :, प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) 
द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुये , केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्द्वारा 
उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 - 2 के अधीन गठित 
केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रौद्योगिक अधि करण , संख्या ( 1 ) , धनबाद 
को न्याय निर्णयन के लिये निर्देशित करती है ।

का० प्रा० 1236 . -- यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है 
कि इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स 
पैच वैली कोल कम्पनी लिमिटेड की चान्दामेटा कोलियरी , डाकघर 
पारसिया , जिला छिन्दवारा के प्रवन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और 
उनके कर्मकारो के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यत , केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनियन 
के लिये निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

अतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुये केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा , 
उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7-- 2 के अधीन गठित 
केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण , जबलपुर को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिये निर्देशित करती है । 

अनुसचो 
क्या मैसर्स पैचवली कम्पनी लिमिटेड की घान्दामेटा 
कोलियरी , डाकघर पारसिया , जिला छिन्दवारा के प्रवन्ध

ऐसी निम्नलिखित शत विनिर्दिष्ट करती है, जिन्हें , एसा व्यक्ति , 
जो उक्त अधिनियम के प्रारम्भ पर भारत में लागत ले कर्म का 
व्यवसाय करता था , अपना नाम रजिस्टर में प्रविष्ट कराने हेतु 
हकदार बनाने के लिए पूरी करेगा , अर्थात् : --- 

1. उसे किसी मान्यताप्राप्त विश्वविद्यालय की इण्टरमीडिएट 
परीक्षा या समतुल्य कोई परीक्षा उत्तीर्ण होना चाहिए । 

2. उसे 28 मई, 1959 के पूर्व 5 वर्ष की अवधि के लिए 
लागत लेखापाल के रूप में मुख्य रूप से लगातार व्यवसाय में होना 
चाहिए । 

3. उसके पास उसके आवेदन की तारीख के ठीक पूर्व 2 
वर्ष के दौरान , प्रतिवर्ष ग्राहक के रूप में औसत 5 समुत्थान होने 
चाहिए और उसके व्यवसाय की व्यापकता पर विचार से हुए , 
5 समुत्थानों में से प्रत्येक में स्थिर आस्तियों के रूप में कुल मिलाकर 
15 लाख रुपए से कम मूल्य को आस्तियों नहीं हों और उस समुत्थान 
में 100 से कम कर्मकार नियोजन में नहीं होने चाहिएं । 

स्पष्टीकरण – ३स अधिसूचना के प्रयोजनों के लिए "समुत्थान " 
से उत्पादन , प्रसंकरण , विनिर्माण या खनन क्रिया - कलापों में लगा 
हुप्रा कोई समुत्थान अभिप्रेत होगा । 

[ सं० 10/ 27 / 70 - आई० जी० सी०]

S . 0 . 1242 - In pursuance of clause ( 11i ) of sub - section 
( 1 ) of section 4 of the Cost and Works Accountants 
Act , 1959 ( 23 of 1959 ) , the Central Government hereby 
specifies the following conditions which a person who, 
at the commencement of the said Act, was engaged in 
the practice of cost accountancy in India , shall fulfi 
to be entitled to have his name entered in the Register , 
namely : 
1. He must have passed the Intermediate Examina 

tion of a recognised University or an equivalent 

examination . 
2 . He must have been mainly in continuous prac 

tice as a Cost Accountant for a period of five 

years prior to the 28th May , 1959 . 
3 . He must have bad as clients five concerns on ar 

average per annum during two years lm 
mediately preceding the date of his application 
and in judging the volume of his practice, each 
of five concerns should have had as fixed assets 
of the aggregate value of not less than rupees 
fifteen lakhs and should have employed not 

less than 100 workers in that concern. 
Explanation .- - The word " concern " shall for the pur 
poses of this notiAcation mean a concern engaged in 
production , processing, manufacturing or mining actlyl

S . O . 1247. - In exercise of the powers conferred by 
clause ( d ) of section 7 of the Customs Act, 1962 ( 52 
of 1962) , the Central Government hereby appoints 
the port at Tondi in the State of Tamil Nadu to Be a 
coastal port for the carrying on of trade in coastal 
goods or any class of such goods with all ports in 
India ,

S . O . 1245. - In exercise of the powers conferred by 
clause ( a ) of section 7, read with sub - section ( 3 ) of 
section 160 , of the Customs Act , 1982 (52 of 1962 ) , the 
Central Government hereby makes the following fur 
ther amendments to the notification of the Govern 
ment of India in the late Central Board of Revenue 
No. 28 - Customs, dated the 2nd April, 1860° namely: --- 

In the Schedule annexed to the said notfication , 
the words " Portonovo " , " Tondi" and " Kulasekarapat 
nam " appearing in the third column against S . Nos . 
2 , 4 and 5 respectively shall be omitted.
